+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ */
+
+#include "config.h"
+
+#include <assert.h>
+#include <errno.h>
+#include <getopt.h>
+#include <locale.h>
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdbool.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include <string.h>
+
+#include "xkbcommon/xkbcommon.h"
+#include "tools-common.h"
+#include "src/utils.h"
+#include "src/keymap-formats.h"
+
+#define DEFAULT_INCLUDE_PATH_PLACEHOLDER "__defaults__"
+
+enum input_format {
+    INPUT_FORMAT_AUTO = 0,
+    INPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO,
+    INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP
+};
+enum output_format {
+    OUTPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP = 0,
+    OUTPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO,
+    OUTPUT_FORMAT_KCCGST,
+    OUTPUT_FORMAT_KCCGST_YAML,
+    OUTPUT_FORMAT_MODMAPS,
+};
+static bool verbose = false;
+static const char *includes[64] = { 0 };
+static size_t num_includes = 0;
+static bool test = false;
+
+static void
+usage(FILE *file, const char *progname)
+{
+    fprintf(file,
+           "Usage: %s [OPTIONS]\n"
+           "\n"
+           "Compile the given input to a keymap and print it\n"
+           "\n"
+           "General options:\n"
+           " --help\n"
+           "    Print this help and exit\n"
+           " --verbose\n"
+           "    Enable verbose debugging output\n"
+           " --test\n"
+           "    Test compilation but do not print the keymap.\n"
+           "\n"
+           "Input options:\n"
+           " --include\n"
+           "    Add the given path to the include path list. This option is\n"
+           "    order-dependent, include paths given first are searched first.\n"
+           "    If an include path is given, the default include path list is\n"
+           "    not used. Use --include-defaults to add the default include\n"
+           "    paths\n"
+           " --include-defaults\n"
+           "    Add the default set of include directories.\n"
+           "    This option is order-dependent, include paths given first\n"
+           "    are searched first.\n"
+           " --input-format <format>\n"
+           "    The keymap format to use for parsing (default: '%d')\n"
+           " --output-format <format>\n"
+           "    The keymap format to use for serializing (default: '%d')\n"
+           " --format <format>\n"
+           "    The keymap format to use for both parsing and serializing\n"
+           " --no-pretty\n"
+           "    Do not pretty-print when serializing a keymap\n"
+           " --drop-unused\n"
+           "    Disable unused bits serialization\n"
+           " --keymap <file>\n"
+           " --from-xkb <file>\n"
+           "    Load the corresponding XKB file, ignore RMLVO options. If <file>\n"
+           "    is \"-\" or missing, then load from stdin."
+           "    This option must not be used with --kccgst.\n"
+           " --rules <rules>\n"
+           "    The XKB ruleset (default: '%s')\n"
+           " --model <model>\n"
+           "    The XKB model (default: '%s')\n"
+           " --layout <layout>\n"
+           "    The XKB layout (default: '%s')\n"
+           " --variant <variant>\n"
+           "    The XKB layout variant (default: '%s')\n"
+           " --options <options>\n"
+           "    The XKB options (default: '%s')\n"
+           " --enable-environment-names\n"
+           "    Allow to set the default RMLVO values via the following environment variables:\n"
+           "    - XKB_DEFAULT_RULES\n"
+           "    - XKB_DEFAULT_MODEL\n"
+           "    - XKB_DEFAULT_LAYOUT\n"
+           "    - XKB_DEFAULT_VARIANT\n"
+           "    - XKB_DEFAULT_OPTIONS\n"
+           "    Note that this option may affect the default values of the previous options.\n"
+           "    This option must not be used with --keymap.\n"
+           "\n"
+           "Output options:\n"
+           " --kccgst\n"
+           "    Print a keymap which only includes the KcCGST component names instead of the full keymap\n"
+           " --kccgst-yaml\n"
+           "    Print the KcCGST component names in YAML format\n"
+           " --rmlvo\n"
+           "    Print the full RMLVO with the defaults filled in for missing elements, in YAML format\n"
+           " --modmaps\n"
+           "    Print real and virtual key modmaps and modifiers encodings in YAML format\n"
+           "\n",
+           progname,
+           DEFAULT_INPUT_KEYMAP_FORMAT, DEFAULT_OUTPUT_KEYMAP_FORMAT,
+           DEFAULT_XKB_RULES, DEFAULT_XKB_MODEL, DEFAULT_XKB_LAYOUT,
+           DEFAULT_XKB_VARIANT ? DEFAULT_XKB_VARIANT : "<none>",
+           DEFAULT_XKB_OPTIONS ? DEFAULT_XKB_OPTIONS : "<none>");
+}
+
+static inline bool
+is_incompatible_with_keymap_input(enum output_format format)
+{
+    switch (format) {
+        case OUTPUT_FORMAT_KCCGST:
+        case OUTPUT_FORMAT_KCCGST_YAML:
+        case OUTPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO:
+            return true;
+        default:
+            return false;
+    }
+}
+
+static bool
+parse_options(int argc, char **argv,
+              enum input_format *input_format_out,
+              enum output_format *output_format_out,
+              enum xkb_keymap_format *keymap_input_format,
+              enum xkb_keymap_format *keymap_output_format,
+              enum xkb_keymap_serialize_flags *serialize_flags,
+              bool *use_env_names,
+              char **path, struct xkb_rule_names *names)
+{
+    enum input_format input_format = INPUT_FORMAT_AUTO;
+    enum output_format output_format = OUTPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP;
+    enum options {
+        /* General */
+        OPT_VERBOSE,
+        OPT_TEST,
+        /* Input */
+        OPT_INCLUDE,
+        OPT_INCLUDE_DEFAULTS,
+        OPT_KEYMAP,
+        OPT_ENABLE_ENV_NAMES,
+        OPT_KEYMAP_INPUT_FORMAT,
+        OPT_KEYMAP_OUTPUT_FORMAT,
+        OPT_KEYMAP_FORMAT,
+        OPT_KEYMAP_NO_PRETTY,
+        OPT_KEYMAP_DROP_UNUSED,
+        OPT_RULES,
+        OPT_MODEL,
+        OPT_LAYOUT,
+        OPT_VARIANT,
+        OPT_OPTION,
+        /* Output */
+        OPT_KCCGST,
+        OPT_KCCGST_YAML,
+        OPT_RMLVO,
+        OPT_MODMAPS,
+    };
+    static struct option opts[] = {
+        /*
+         * General
+         */
+        {"help",             no_argument,            0, 'h'},
+        {"verbose",          no_argument,            0, OPT_VERBOSE},
+        {"test",             no_argument,            0, OPT_TEST},
+        /*
+         * Input
+         */
+        {"include",          required_argument,      0, OPT_INCLUDE},
+        {"include-defaults", no_argument,            0, OPT_INCLUDE_DEFAULTS},
+        {"keymap",           optional_argument,      0, OPT_KEYMAP},
+        /* Alias maintained for backward compatibility */
+        {"from-xkb",         optional_argument,      0, OPT_KEYMAP},
+        {"enable-environment-names", no_argument,    0, OPT_ENABLE_ENV_NAMES},
+        {"input-format",     required_argument,      0, OPT_KEYMAP_INPUT_FORMAT},
+        {"output-format",    required_argument,      0, OPT_KEYMAP_OUTPUT_FORMAT},
+        {"format",           required_argument,      0, OPT_KEYMAP_FORMAT},
+        {"no-pretty",        no_argument,            0, OPT_KEYMAP_NO_PRETTY},
+        {"drop-unused",      no_argument,            0, OPT_KEYMAP_DROP_UNUSED},
+        {"rules",            required_argument,      0, OPT_RULES},
+        {"model",            required_argument,      0, OPT_MODEL},
+        {"layout",           required_argument,      0, OPT_LAYOUT},
+        {"variant",          required_argument,      0, OPT_VARIANT},
+        {"options",          required_argument,      0, OPT_OPTION},
+        /*
+         * Output
+         */
+        {"kccgst",           no_argument,            0, OPT_KCCGST},
+        {"kccgst-yaml",      no_argument,            0, OPT_KCCGST_YAML},
+        {"rmlvo",            no_argument,            0, OPT_RMLVO},
+        {"modmaps",          no_argument,            0, OPT_MODMAPS},
+        {0, 0, 0, 0},
+    };
+
+    *use_env_names = false;
+    int option_index = 0;
+    while (1) {
+        option_index = 0;
+        int c = getopt_long(argc, argv, "h", opts, &option_index);
+        if (c == -1)
+            break;
+
+        switch (c) {
+        /* General */
+        case 'h':
+            usage(stdout, argv[0]);
+            exit(0);
+        case OPT_VERBOSE:
+            verbose = true;
+            break;
+        case OPT_TEST:
+            test = true;
+            break;
+        /* Input */
+        case OPT_INCLUDE:
+            if (num_includes >= ARRAY_SIZE(includes))
+                goto too_many_includes;
+            includes[num_includes++] = optarg;
+            break;
+        case OPT_INCLUDE_DEFAULTS:
+            if (num_includes >= ARRAY_SIZE(includes))
+                goto too_many_includes;
+            includes[num_includes++] = DEFAULT_INCLUDE_PATH_PLACEHOLDER;
+            break;
+        case OPT_KEYMAP:
+            if (*use_env_names)
+                goto keymap_env_error;
+            if (input_format == INPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO)
+                goto input_format_error;
+            if (is_incompatible_with_keymap_input(output_format))
+                goto output_incompatible_with_keymap_input_error;
+            input_format = INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP;
+            /* Optional arguments require `=`, but we want to make this
+             * requirement optional too, so that both `--keymap=xxx` and
+             * `--keymap xxx` work. */
+            if (!optarg && argv[optind] &&
+                (argv[optind][0] != '-' || strcmp(argv[optind], "-") == 0 )) {
+                *path = argv[optind++];
+            } else {
+                *path = optarg;
+            }
+            break;
+        case OPT_ENABLE_ENV_NAMES:
+            if (input_format == INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP)
+                goto keymap_env_error;
+            *use_env_names = true;
+            input_format = INPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO;
+            break;
+        case OPT_KEYMAP_INPUT_FORMAT:
+            *keymap_input_format = xkb_keymap_parse_format(optarg);
+            if (!(*keymap_input_format)) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: invalid --input-format: \"%s\"\n", optarg);
+                usage(stderr, argv[0]);
+                exit(EXIT_INVALID_USAGE);
+            }
+            break;
+        case OPT_KEYMAP_OUTPUT_FORMAT:
+            *keymap_output_format = xkb_keymap_parse_format(optarg);
+            if (!(*keymap_output_format)) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: invalid --output-format: \"%s\"\n", optarg);
+                usage(stderr, argv[0]);
+                exit(EXIT_INVALID_USAGE);
+            }
+            break;
+        case OPT_KEYMAP_FORMAT:
+            *keymap_input_format = xkb_keymap_parse_format(optarg);
+            if (!(*keymap_input_format)) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: invalid --format: \"%s\"\n", optarg);
+                usage(stderr, argv[0]);
+                exit(EXIT_INVALID_USAGE);
+            }
+            *keymap_output_format = *keymap_input_format;
+            break;
+        case OPT_KEYMAP_NO_PRETTY:
+            *serialize_flags &= ~XKB_KEYMAP_SERIALIZE_PRETTY;
+            break;
+        case OPT_KEYMAP_DROP_UNUSED:
+            *serialize_flags &= ~XKB_KEYMAP_SERIALIZE_KEEP_UNUSED;
+            break;
+        case OPT_RULES:
+            if (input_format == INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP)
+                goto input_format_error;
+            names->rules = optarg;
+            input_format = INPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO;
+            break;
+        case OPT_MODEL:
+            if (input_format == INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP)
+                goto input_format_error;
+            names->model = optarg;
+            input_format = INPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO;
+            break;
+        case OPT_LAYOUT:
+            if (input_format == INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP)
+                goto input_format_error;
+            names->layout = optarg;
+            input_format = INPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO;
+            break;
+        case OPT_VARIANT:
+            if (input_format == INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP)
+                goto input_format_error;
+            names->variant = optarg;
+            input_format = INPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO;
+            break;
+        case OPT_OPTION:
+            if (input_format == INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP)
+                goto input_format_error;
+            names->options = optarg;
+            input_format = INPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO;
+            break;
+        /* Output */
+        case OPT_KCCGST:
+            assert(is_incompatible_with_keymap_input(OUTPUT_FORMAT_KCCGST));
+            if (input_format == INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP)
+                goto output_incompatible_with_keymap_input_error;
+            if (output_format != OUTPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP &&
+                output_format != OUTPUT_FORMAT_KCCGST)
+                goto output_format_error;
+            output_format = OUTPUT_FORMAT_KCCGST;
+            break;
+        case OPT_KCCGST_YAML:
+            assert(is_incompatible_with_keymap_input(OUTPUT_FORMAT_KCCGST_YAML));
+            if (input_format == INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP)
+                goto output_incompatible_with_keymap_input_error;
+            if (output_format != OUTPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP &&
+                output_format != OUTPUT_FORMAT_KCCGST_YAML)
+                goto output_format_error;
+            output_format = OUTPUT_FORMAT_KCCGST_YAML;
+            break;
+        case OPT_RMLVO:
+            assert(is_incompatible_with_keymap_input(OUTPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO));
+            if (input_format == INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP)
+                goto output_incompatible_with_keymap_input_error;
+            if (output_format != OUTPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP &&
+                output_format != OUTPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO)
+                goto output_format_error;
+            output_format = OUTPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO;
+            break;
+        case OPT_MODMAPS:
+            assert(!is_incompatible_with_keymap_input(OUTPUT_FORMAT_MODMAPS));
+            if (output_format != OUTPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP &&
+                output_format != OUTPUT_FORMAT_MODMAPS)
+                goto output_format_error;
+            output_format = OUTPUT_FORMAT_MODMAPS;
+            break;
+        default:
+            goto invalid_usage;
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (optind < argc && !isempty(argv[optind])) {
+        /* Some positional arguments left: use as a keymap input */
+        if (input_format != INPUT_FORMAT_AUTO ||
+            is_incompatible_with_keymap_input(output_format)) {
+            goto too_much_arguments;
+        }
+        input_format = INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP;
+        *path = argv[optind++];
+        if (optind < argc) {
+            /* Further positional arguments is an error */
+too_much_arguments:
+            fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: Too many positional arguments\n");
+            goto invalid_usage;
+        }
+    } else if (is_pipe_or_regular_file(STDIN_FILENO) &&
+               input_format != INPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO &&
+               !is_incompatible_with_keymap_input(output_format)) {
+        /* No positional argument: detect piping */
+        input_format = INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP;
+    }
+
+    if (isempty(*path) || strcmp(*path, "-") == 0)
+        *path = NULL;
+
+    *input_format_out = input_format;
+    *output_format_out = output_format;
+    return true;
+
+input_format_error:
+    fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: Cannot use RMLVO options with keymap input\n");
+    goto invalid_usage;
+
+keymap_env_error:
+    fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: --keymap is not compatible with "
+                    "--enable-environment-names\n");
+    goto invalid_usage;
+
+output_format_error:
+    fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: Cannot mix output formats\n");
+    goto invalid_usage;
+
+output_incompatible_with_keymap_input_error:
+    fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: Output format incompatible with keymap input\n");
+    goto invalid_usage;
+
+too_many_includes:
+    fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: too many includes (max: %zu)\n",
+            ARRAY_SIZE(includes));
+
+invalid_usage:
+    usage(stderr, argv[0]);
+    exit(EXIT_INVALID_USAGE);
+}
+
+static int
+print_rmlvo(struct xkb_context *ctx, struct xkb_rule_names *rmlvo)
+{
+    /* Resolve default RMLVO values */
+    struct xkb_rule_names resolved = { NULL };
+    xkb_components_names_from_rules(ctx, rmlvo, &resolved, NULL);
+
+    if (test)
+        return EXIT_SUCCESS;
+
+    printf("rules: \"%s\"\nmodel: \"%s\"\nlayout: \"%s\"\nvariant: \"%s\"\n"
+           "options: \"%s\"\n",
+           resolved.rules, resolved.model, resolved.layout,
+           resolved.variant ? resolved.variant : "",
+           resolved.options ? resolved.options : "");
+    return EXIT_SUCCESS;
+}
+
+static int
+print_kccgst(struct xkb_context *ctx, struct xkb_rule_names *rmlvo, bool yaml)
+{
+        struct xkb_component_names kccgst = { 0 };
+
+        /* Resolve missing RMLVO values, then resolve the RMLVO names to
+         * KcCGST components */
+        if (!xkb_components_names_from_rules(ctx, rmlvo, NULL, &kccgst))
+            return EXIT_FAILURE;
+        if (test)
+            goto out;
+
+        if (yaml) {
+            printf("keycodes: \"%s\"\n"
+                   "types: \"%s\"\n"
+                   "compat: \"%s\"\n"
+                   "symbols: \"%s\"\n",
+                   kccgst.keycodes, kccgst.types, kccgst.compatibility,
+                   kccgst.symbols);
+            /* Contrary to the previous components, geometry can be empty */
+            if (!isempty(kccgst.geometry)) {
+                printf("geometry: \"%s\"\n", kccgst.geometry);
+            }
+        } else {
+            printf("xkb_keymap {\n"
+                   "  xkb_keycodes { include \"%s\" };\n"
+                   "  xkb_types { include \"%s\" };\n"
+                   "  xkb_compat { include \"%s\" };\n"
+                   "  xkb_symbols { include \"%s\" };\n",
+                   kccgst.keycodes, kccgst.types, kccgst.compatibility,
+                   kccgst.symbols);
+            /* Contrary to the previous components, geometry can be empty */
+            if (!isempty(kccgst.geometry)) {
+                printf("  xkb_geometry { include \"%s\" };\n", kccgst.geometry);
+            }
+            printf("};\n");
+        }
+out:
+        free(kccgst.keycodes);
+        free(kccgst.types);
+        free(kccgst.compatibility);
+        free(kccgst.symbols);
+        free(kccgst.geometry);
+
+        return EXIT_SUCCESS;
+}
+
+static struct xkb_keymap*
+load_keymap(struct xkb_context *ctx, enum xkb_keymap_format keymap_input_format,
+            enum input_format format, const struct xkb_rule_names *rmlvo,
+            const char *path)
+{
+    if (format == INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP) {
+        FILE *file = NULL;
+        if (path) {
+            /* Read from regular file */
+            file = fopen(path, "rb");
+        } else {
+            /* Read from stdin */
+            file = tools_read_stdin();
+        }
+        if (!file) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: Failed to open keymap file \"%s\": %s\n",
+                    path ? path : "stdin", strerror(errno));
+            return NULL;
+        }
+        return xkb_keymap_new_from_file(ctx, file,
+                                        keymap_input_format,
+                                        XKB_KEYMAP_COMPILE_NO_FLAGS);
+    } else {
+        return xkb_keymap_new_from_names2(ctx, rmlvo, keymap_input_format,
+                                          XKB_KEYMAP_COMPILE_NO_FLAGS);
+    }
+}
+
+static int
+print_keymap(struct xkb_context *ctx,
+             enum xkb_keymap_format keymap_input_format,
+             enum xkb_keymap_format keymap_output_format,
+             enum input_format format, enum xkb_keymap_serialize_flags flags,
+             const struct xkb_rule_names *rmlvo,
+             const char *path)
+{
+    int ret = EXIT_SUCCESS;
+    struct xkb_keymap *keymap = load_keymap(ctx, keymap_input_format,
+                                            format, rmlvo, path);
+
+    if (!keymap) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: Couldn't create xkb keymap\n");
+        ret = EXIT_FAILURE;
+    } else if (!test) {
+        char* keymap_string =
+            xkb_keymap_get_as_string2(keymap, keymap_output_format, flags);
+        if (!keymap_string) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: Couldn't get the keymap string\n");
+        } else {
+            fputs(keymap_string, stdout);
+            free(keymap_string);
+        }
+    }
+    xkb_keymap_unref(keymap);
+    return ret;
+}
+
+static int
+print_modmaps(struct xkb_context *ctx,
+              enum xkb_keymap_format keymap_input_format,
+              enum input_format format, const struct xkb_rule_names *rmlvo,
+              const char *path)
+{
+    int ret = EXIT_SUCCESS;
+    struct xkb_keymap *keymap = load_keymap(ctx, keymap_input_format,
+                                            format, rmlvo, path);
+    if (!keymap) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: Couldn't create xkb keymap\n");
+        ret = EXIT_FAILURE;
+    } else if (!test) {
+        print_modifiers_encodings(keymap);
+        printf("\n");
+        print_keys_modmaps(keymap);
+    }
+    xkb_keymap_unref(keymap);
+    return ret;
+}
+
+int
+main(int argc, char **argv)
+{
+    struct xkb_context *ctx;
+    char *keymap_path = NULL;
+    struct xkb_rule_names names = { 0 };
+    bool use_env_names = false;
+    enum xkb_keymap_format keymap_input_format = DEFAULT_INPUT_KEYMAP_FORMAT;
+    enum xkb_keymap_format keymap_output_format = DEFAULT_OUTPUT_KEYMAP_FORMAT;
+    enum xkb_keymap_serialize_flags serialize_flags =
+        (enum xkb_keymap_serialize_flags) DEFAULT_KEYMAP_SERIALIZE_FLAGS;
+    int rc = 1;
+
+    setlocale(LC_ALL, "");
+
+    if (argc < 1) {
+        usage(stderr, argv[0]);
+        return EXIT_INVALID_USAGE;
+    }
+
+    enum input_format input_format = INPUT_FORMAT_AUTO;
+    enum output_format output_format = OUTPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP;
+    if (!parse_options(argc, argv, &input_format, &output_format,
+                       &keymap_input_format, &keymap_output_format,
+                       &serialize_flags, &use_env_names, &keymap_path, &names))
+        return EXIT_INVALID_USAGE;
+
+    enum xkb_context_flags ctx_flags = XKB_CONTEXT_NO_DEFAULT_INCLUDES;
+    if (!use_env_names)
+        ctx_flags |= XKB_CONTEXT_NO_ENVIRONMENT_NAMES;
+
+    ctx = xkb_context_new(ctx_flags);
+    assert(ctx);
+
+    if (verbose)
+        tools_enable_verbose_logging(ctx);
+
+    if (num_includes == 0)
+        includes[num_includes++] = DEFAULT_INCLUDE_PATH_PLACEHOLDER;
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < num_includes; i++) {
+        const char *include = includes[i];
+        if (strcmp(include, DEFAULT_INCLUDE_PATH_PLACEHOLDER) == 0)
+            xkb_context_include_path_append_default(ctx);
+        else
+            xkb_context_include_path_append(ctx, include);
+    }
+
+    switch (output_format) {
+    case OUTPUT_FORMAT_RMLVO:
+        assert(input_format != INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP);
+        rc = print_rmlvo(ctx, &names);
+        break;
+    case OUTPUT_FORMAT_KCCGST:
+        assert(input_format != INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP);
+        rc = print_kccgst(ctx, &names, false);
+        break;
+    case OUTPUT_FORMAT_KCCGST_YAML:
+        assert(input_format != INPUT_FORMAT_KEYMAP);
+        rc = print_kccgst(ctx, &names, true);
+        break;
+    case OUTPUT_FORMAT_MODMAPS:
+        rc = print_modmaps(ctx, keymap_input_format,
+                           input_format, &names, keymap_path);
+        break;
+    default:
+        rc = print_keymap(ctx, keymap_input_format, keymap_output_format,
+                          input_format, serialize_flags, &names, keymap_path);
+    }
+
+    xkb_context_unref(ctx);
+
+    return rc;
+}

+/*
+ * Copyright © 2009 Dan Nicholson
+ * Copyright © 2012 Intel Corporation
+ * Copyright © 2012 Ran Benita <ran234@gmail.com>
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Author: Dan Nicholson <dbn.lists@gmail.com>
+ * Author: Ran Benita <ran234@gmail.com>
+ * Author: Daniel Stone <daniel@fooishbar.org>
+ */
+
+#include "config.h"
+
+#include <stdbool.h>
+
+#include "darray.h"
+#include "utils.h"
+#include "xkbcommon/xkbcommon.h"
+#include "xkbcomp-priv.h"
+#include "keymap.h"
+#include "rules.h"
+#include "rmlvo.h"
+
+bool
+xkb_components_names_from_rules(struct xkb_context *ctx,
+                                const struct xkb_rule_names *rmlvo_in,
+                                struct xkb_rule_names *rmlvo_out,
+                                struct xkb_component_names *components_out)
+{
+    /* Resolve default RMLVO values. We need a mutable copy of the input. */
+    struct xkb_rule_names rmlvo = *rmlvo_in;
+    xkb_context_sanitize_rule_names(ctx, &rmlvo);
+
+    if (rmlvo_out) {
+        /* Copy the sanitized RMLVO, if required. */
+        *rmlvo_out = rmlvo;
+    }
+    if (!components_out) {
+        /* KcCGST not required.
+         * While RMLVO and KcCGST outputs are both optional, at least one must
+         * be provided. */
+        return !!rmlvo_out;
+    }
+
+    /* Resolve the RMLVO names to KcCGST components */
+    *components_out = (struct xkb_component_names){ 0 };
+    return xkb_components_from_rules_names(ctx, &rmlvo, components_out, NULL);
+}
+
+static bool
+compile_keymap_file(struct xkb_keymap *keymap, XkbFile *file)
+{
+    if (file->file_type != FILE_TYPE_KEYMAP) {
+        log_err(keymap->ctx, XKB_ERROR_KEYMAP_COMPILATION_FAILED,
+                "Cannot compile a %s file alone into a keymap\n",
+                xkb_file_type_to_string(file->file_type));
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    if (!CompileKeymap(file, keymap)) {
+        log_err(keymap->ctx, XKB_ERROR_KEYMAP_COMPILATION_FAILED,
+                "Failed to compile keymap\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    return true;
+}
+
+static bool
+text_v1_keymap_new_from_rmlvo(struct xkb_keymap *keymap,
+                              const struct xkb_rmlvo_builder *rmlvo)
+{
+    bool ok;
+    struct xkb_component_names kccgst;
+    XkbFile *file;
+
+    if (keymap->ctx->log_level >= XKB_LOG_LEVEL_DEBUG) {
+        struct xkb_rule_names names = { 0 };
+        const size_t buf_size = sizeof(rmlvo->ctx->text_buffer) - 1;
+        char *buf = xkb_context_get_buffer(rmlvo->ctx, buf_size);
+        if (unlikely(!buf))
+            return false;
+        ok = xkb_rmlvo_builder_to_rules_names(rmlvo, &names, buf, buf_size);
+        if (unlikely(!ok))
+            return false;
+        log_dbg(keymap->ctx, XKB_LOG_MESSAGE_NO_ID,
+                "Compiling from RMLVO builder: rules '%s', model '%s', layout '%s', "
+                "variant '%s', options '%s'\n",
+                names.rules, names.model, names.layout, names.variant,
+                names.options);
+    }
+
+    /* Resolve the RMLVO components to KcCGST components and get the
+     * expected number of layouts */
+    ok = xkb_components_from_rmlvo_builder(rmlvo, &kccgst, &keymap->num_groups);
+    if (!ok) {
+        struct xkb_rule_names names = { 0 };
+        const size_t buf_size = sizeof(rmlvo->ctx->text_buffer);
+        char *buf = xkb_context_get_buffer(rmlvo->ctx, buf_size);
+        if (unlikely(!buf))
+            return false;
+        ok = xkb_rmlvo_builder_to_rules_names(rmlvo, &names, buf, buf_size);
+        if (unlikely(!ok))
+            return false;
+        log_err(keymap->ctx, XKB_ERROR_KEYMAP_COMPILATION_FAILED,
+                "Couldn't look up rules '%s', model '%s', layout '%s', "
+                "variant '%s', options '%s'\n",
+                names.rules, names.model, names.layout, names.variant,
+                names.options);
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    const xkb_layout_index_t max_groups = format_max_groups(keymap->format);
+    if (keymap->num_groups > max_groups)
+        keymap->num_groups = max_groups;
+
+    log_dbg(keymap->ctx, XKB_LOG_MESSAGE_NO_ID,
+            "Compiling from KcCGST: keycodes '%s', types '%s', "
+            "compat '%s', symbols '%s'\n",
+            kccgst.keycodes, kccgst.types, kccgst.compatibility, kccgst.symbols);
+
+    file = XkbFileFromComponents(keymap->ctx, &kccgst);
+
+    free(kccgst.keycodes);
+    free(kccgst.types);
+    free(kccgst.compatibility);
+    free(kccgst.symbols);
+    free(kccgst.geometry);
+
+    if (!file) {
+        log_err(keymap->ctx, XKB_ERROR_KEYMAP_COMPILATION_FAILED,
+                "Failed to generate parsed XKB file from components\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    ok = compile_keymap_file(keymap, file);
+    FreeXkbFile(file);
+    return ok;
+}
+
+static bool
+text_v1_keymap_new_from_names(struct xkb_keymap *keymap,
+                              const struct xkb_rule_names *rmlvo)
+{
+    bool ok;
+    struct xkb_component_names kccgst;
+    XkbFile *file;
+
+    log_dbg(keymap->ctx, XKB_LOG_MESSAGE_NO_ID,
+            "Compiling from RMLVO: rules '%s', model '%s', layout '%s', "
+            "variant '%s', options '%s'\n",
+            rmlvo->rules, rmlvo->model, rmlvo->layout, rmlvo->variant,
+            rmlvo->options);
+
+    /* Resolve the RMLVO components to KcCGST components and get the
+     * expected number of layouts */
+    ok = xkb_components_from_rules_names(keymap->ctx, rmlvo, &kccgst,
+                                   &keymap->num_groups);
+    if (!ok) {
+        log_err(keymap->ctx, XKB_ERROR_KEYMAP_COMPILATION_FAILED,
+                "Couldn't look up rules '%s', model '%s', layout '%s', "
+                "variant '%s', options '%s'\n",
+                rmlvo->rules, rmlvo->model, rmlvo->layout, rmlvo->variant,
+                rmlvo->options);
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    const xkb_layout_index_t max_groups = format_max_groups(keymap->format);
+    if (keymap->num_groups > max_groups)
+        keymap->num_groups = max_groups;
+
+    log_dbg(keymap->ctx, XKB_LOG_MESSAGE_NO_ID,
+            "Compiling from KcCGST: keycodes '%s', types '%s', "
+            "compat '%s', symbols '%s'\n",
+            kccgst.keycodes, kccgst.types, kccgst.compatibility, kccgst.symbols);
+
+    file = XkbFileFromComponents(keymap->ctx, &kccgst);
+
+    free(kccgst.keycodes);
+    free(kccgst.types);
+    free(kccgst.compatibility);
+    free(kccgst.symbols);
+    free(kccgst.geometry);
+
+    if (!file) {
+        log_err(keymap->ctx, XKB_ERROR_KEYMAP_COMPILATION_FAILED,
+                "Failed to generate parsed XKB file from components\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    ok = compile_keymap_file(keymap, file);
+    FreeXkbFile(file);
+    return ok;
+}
+
+static bool
+text_v1_keymap_new_from_string(struct xkb_keymap *keymap,
+                               const char *string, size_t len)
+{
+    bool ok;
+    XkbFile *xkb_file;
+
+    xkb_file = XkbParseString(keymap->ctx, string, len, "(input string)", NULL);
+    if (!xkb_file) {
+        log_err(keymap->ctx, XKB_ERROR_KEYMAP_COMPILATION_FAILED,
+                "Failed to parse input xkb string\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    ok = compile_keymap_file(keymap, xkb_file);
+    FreeXkbFile(xkb_file);
+    return ok;
+}
+
+static bool
+text_v1_keymap_new_from_file(struct xkb_keymap *keymap, FILE *file)
+{
+    bool ok;
+    XkbFile *xkb_file;
+
+    xkb_file = XkbParseFile(keymap->ctx, file, "(unknown file)", NULL);
+    if (!xkb_file) {
+        log_err(keymap->ctx, XKB_ERROR_KEYMAP_COMPILATION_FAILED,
+                "Failed to parse input xkb file\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    ok = compile_keymap_file(keymap, xkb_file);
+    FreeXkbFile(xkb_file);
+    return ok;
+}
+
+const struct xkb_keymap_format_ops text_v1_keymap_format_ops = {
+    .keymap_new_from_rmlvo = text_v1_keymap_new_from_rmlvo,
+    .keymap_new_from_names = text_v1_keymap_new_from_names,
+    .keymap_new_from_string = text_v1_keymap_new_from_string,
+    .keymap_new_from_file = text_v1_keymap_new_from_file,
+    .keymap_get_as_string = text_v1_keymap_get_as_string,
+};

+//
+//  Rules for resolving XKB components for use with XFree86
+//  Copyright 1996 by Joseph Moss
+//
+//  2002 Modifier: Ivan Pascal      The XFree86 Project

+
+// PC models
+! $pcmodels = abnt2 jp106 kr106 pc86 pc101 pc102 pc104 pc104alt pc105
+
+// Jolla devices and keyboards
+! $jollamodels = jollasbj
+
+// Microsoft models (using MS geometry)
+! $msmodels = microsoft microsoft4000 microsoft7000 microsoftpro \
+              microsoftprose microsoftprousb microsoftsurface
+
+// TypeMatrix geometries
+! $tmgeometries = tm2020 tm2030PS2 tm2030USB tm2030USB-102 tm2030USB-106
+
+// Country files that provide specializations for the OLPC:
+! $olpclayouts = af am ara br ca es et fr in it kh kz mn ng np pk ru th tr us
+
+! $applealu = applealu_ansi applealu_iso applealu_jis
+
+! $macvendorlayouts = ch de dk fi fr gb is it latam nl no pt se us
+
+// Macintosh vendor: country-specific variants
+! $mac_ch_variants = de fr nodeadkeys
+! $mac_de_variants = nodeadkeys Sundeadkeys sundeadkeys
+! $mac_dk_variants = macbookpro nodeadkeys
+! $mac_pt_variants = nodeadkeys Sundeadkeys sundeadkeys
+
+// The countries that predominantly have AZERTY or QWERTZ layouts:
+! $azerty = be fr
+! $qwertz = al ch cz de hr hu ro si sk
+
+! $thinkpads = thinkpad thinkpad60 thinkpadz60
+
+! $sun = sun_type6_euro_usb sun_type6_jp sun_type6_jp_usb sun_type6_unix_usb \
+         sun_type6_usb sun_type7_euro_usb sun_type7_jp_usb sun_type7_unix_usb \
+         sun_type7_usb
+
+! $sun_jp = sun_type6_jp sun_type6_jp_usb sun_type7_jp_usb
+
+// Sun Type_6_7 keyboards with custom layouts
+! $sun_custom = ara be br ca ch cz de dk ee es fi fr gb gr it jp kr lt lv nl \
+                no pl pt ro ru se sk tr tw ua us
+
+! $sun_var = basic sun_type6 sun_type6_de sun_type6_fr sun_type6_suncompat \
+             sun_type7 sun_type7_suncompat suncompat
+
+! $sun_compat = sun_type6 sun_type6_suncompat sun_type7_suncompat suncompat
+
+! model		=	keycodes
+ $jollamodels	=	evdev+jolla(jolla)
+  olpc		=	evdev+olpc(olpc)
+  olpcm		=	evdev+olpc(olpcm)
+  *		=	evdev
+

+ $azerty	=	+aliases(azerty)
+ $qwertz	=	+aliases(qwertz)
+  *		=	+aliases(qwerty)
+
+! layout[1]	=	keycodes
+ $azerty	=	+aliases(azerty)

+  *		=	+aliases(qwerty)
+
+! model		layout	=	geometry
+  thinkpad	us	=	thinkpad(us)
+
+! model			=	geometry
+  microsoftelite	=	microsoft(elite)
+ $msmodels		=	microsoft(natural)
+  dell101		=	dell(dell101)
+  dellm65		=	dell(dellm65)

+  hp6000		=	hp(omnibook)

+  hpdv5			=	hp(dv5)

+  sanwaskbkg3		=	sanwa(sanwaskbkg3)

+  thinkpadz60		=	thinkpad(60)

+! model		 layout			 variant		=	symbols
+  *		 ben			 basic			=	pc+in(ben)
+  *		 ben			 probhat		=	pc+in(ben_probhat)
+  *		 dev			 basic			=	pc+in(deva)
+  *		 guj			 basic			=	pc+in(guj)
+  *		 gur			 basic			=	pc+in(guru)
+  *		 kan			 basic			=	pc+in(kan)
+  *		 mal			 basic			=	pc+in(mal)
+  *		 mao			 basic			=	pc+nz(mao)
+  *		 ogham			 basic			=	pc+ie(ogam)
+  *		 ogham			 is434			=	pc+ie(ogam_is434)
+  *		 ori			 basic			=	pc+in(ori)
+  *		 syr			 basic			=	pc+sy(syc)
+  *		 syr			 phonetic		=	pc+sy(syc_phonetic)

+# Files that are a straight install
+install_data('README', 'xkb.dtd', 'xfree98',
+             install_dir: dir_xkb_rules)
+
+base_xml = files('base.xml')
+base_extras_xml = files('base.extras.xml')
+
+MINIMUM_PYTHON_VERSION = '3.11'
+pymod = import('python')
+python = pymod.find_installation('python3')
+if python.language_version().version_compare('<@0@'.format(MINIMUM_PYTHON_VERSION))
+    error('Minimum required Python version: @0@, but got: @1@'.format(
+        MINIMUM_PYTHON_VERSION,
+        python.language_version()
+    ))
+endif
+xml2lst = find_program('xml2lst.pl')
+rules_template = files('rules.in')
+rules_files = []
+registry_files = []
+foreach ruleset: ['base', 'evdev']
+    # First: generate the "evdev" and "base" rules files
+    cmd = [
+        'python3',
+        '-m',
+        'rules.generator',
+        'rules',
+        '--ruleset',
+        ruleset,
+        '--version',
+        'v2',
+        '--output',
+        meson.current_build_dir() / ruleset
+    ]
+    if get_option('compat-rules')
+        cmd += ['--compat']
+    endif
+    cmd += rules_template
+    rules_files += custom_target(f'rules-@ruleset@',
+                                 build_by_default: true,
+                                 command: cmd,
+                                 depend_files: rules_template,
+                                 env: { 'PYTHONPATH': meson.project_source_root() },
+                                 output: ruleset,
+                                 install: true,
+                                 install_dir: dir_xkb_rules)
+
+    # Second: generate the xml files, simply copied from the base*.xml files
+    ruleset_xml = configure_file(output: f'@ruleset@.xml',
+                                 input: base_xml,
+                                 copy: true,
+                                 install: true,
+                                 install_dir: dir_xkb_rules)
+    registry_files += ruleset_xml
+    # This is used by the man page's meson.build
+    if ruleset == 'evdev'
+      evdev_ruleset = ruleset_xml
+    endif
+    registry_files += configure_file(output: f'@ruleset@.extras.xml',
+                                     input: base_extras_xml,
+                                     copy: true,
+                                     install: true,
+                                     install_dir: dir_xkb_rules)
+
+    # Third: generate the evdev.lst and base.lst files
+    lst_file = f'@ruleset@.lst'
+    custom_target(lst_file,
+                  build_by_default: true,
+                  command: [xml2lst, ruleset_xml],
+                  capture: true,
+                  output: lst_file,
+                  install: true,
+                  install_dir: dir_xkb_rules)
+endforeach
+
+if get_option('xorg-rules-symlinks')
+  foreach suffix: ['', '.lst', '.xml']
+    if meson.version().version_compare('>= 0.61')
+      install_symlink('xorg' + suffix,
+                      pointing_to: 'base' + suffix,
+                      install_dir: dir_xkb_rules)
+    else
+      meson.add_install_script('sh', '-c',
+        'ln -s base@0@ $DESTDIR@1@/xorg@0@'.format(suffix, dir_xkb_rules))
+    endif
+  endforeach
+endif
+
+# Copy the DTD to the build directory, the man page generation expects it in
+# the same directory as the input XML file.
+configure_file(output: 'xkb.dtd',
+               input: 'xkb.dtd',
+               copy: true,
+               install: false)

+################################################################################
+### This file use the following template format:
+### - Lines starting with “#” are template syntax:
+###   - Comments are introduced using “###”, as illustrated in the current line.
+###   - The second character of the line is ignored.
+###   - The rest of the line is interpreted as Python syntax.
+###   - Indentation is 4 spaces.
+###   - Indented blocks of code must be terminated by the corresponding
+###     “endXXX” instruction, e.g.:
+###
+###         if a:
+###             ...
+###         else:
+###             ...
+###         endif
+###
+### - Lines not starting with “#” are interpreted as a Python f-strings.
+###
+###   Whenever possible, prefer using layout *ranges* than layout indexes, such
+###   as: `single`, `first`, `later` and `any`. They are documented at:
+###   https://xkbcommon.org/doc/current/rule-file-format.html#grammar
+###   They will be converted to the appropriate syntax when rendering in the
+###   legacy format.
+################################################################################

+// Keyboard layouts for Brazil.
+
+default
+xkb_symbols "abnt2" {
+
+    // Based on a very simple Brazilian ABNT2 keyboard,
+    // by Ricardo Y. Igarashi (iga@that.com.br).
+    // With added support for dead keys in I18N applications,
+    // by Conectiva  (http://www.conectiva.com.br).
+
+    include "latin"
+    name[Group1]="Portuguese (Brazil)";
+
+    key <TLDE> { [   apostrophe,       quotedbl,       notsign,         notsign ] };
+    key <AE02> { [            2,             at,   twosuperior,         onehalf ] };
+    key <AE03> { [            3,     numbersign, threesuperior,   threequarters ] };
+    key <AE04> { [            4,         dollar,      sterling,      onequarter ] };
+    key <AE05> { [            5,        percent,          cent,    threeeighths ] };
+    key <AE06> { [            6, dead_diaeresis,       notsign,       diaeresis ] };
+    key <AE12> { [        equal,           plus,       section,     dead_ogonek ] };
+
+    key <AD01> { [            q,              Q,         slash,           slash ] };
+    key <AD02> { [            w,              W,      question,        question ] };
+    key <AD03> { [            e,              E,        degree,          degree ] };
+    key <AD04> { [            r,              R,    registered,      registered ] };
+    key <AD11> { [   dead_acute,     dead_grave,         acute,           grave ] };
+    key <AD12> { [  bracketleft,      braceleft,   ordfeminine,     dead_macron ] };
+    key <BKSL> { [ bracketright,     braceright,     masculine,       masculine ] };
+
+    key <AC10> { [     ccedilla,       Ccedilla,    dead_acute,dead_doubleacute ] };
+    key <AC11> { [   dead_tilde,dead_circumflex,    asciitilde,     asciicircum ] };
+
+    key <LSGT> { [    backslash,            bar,    dead_caron,      dead_breve ] };
+    key <AB03> { [            c,              C,     copyright,       copyright ] };
+    key <AB07> { [            m,              M,            mu,              mu ] };
+    key <AB10> { [    semicolon,          colon, dead_belowdot,   dead_abovedot ] };
+
+    // The ABNT-2 keyboard has this special key:
+    key <AB11> { [        slash,       question,        degree,    questiondown ] };
+
+    include "kpdl(comma)"
+
+    include "level3(ralt_switch)"
+};

+//
+// Brazilian Dvorak layout                                 2005-04-18

+partial alphanumeric_keys
+xkb_symbols "dvorak" {
+
+    name[Group1]="Portuguese (Brazil, Dvorak)";
+
+// Numeric row
+    key <TLDE> { [   apostrophe,        quotedbl,           dead_caron, dead_doubleacute ] };
+    key <AE01> { [            1,          exclam,          onesuperior,       exclamdown ] };
+    key <AE02> { [            2,              at,          twosuperior,          onehalf ] };
+    key <AE03> { [            3,      numbersign,        threesuperior,    threequarters ] };
+    key <AE04> { [            4,          dollar,             sterling,       onequarter ] };
+    key <AE05> { [            5,         percent,                 cent,         permille ] };
+    key <AE06> { [            6,  dead_diaeresis,              notsign,        diaeresis ] };
+    key <AE07> { [            7,       ampersand,        dead_belowdot,    dead_abovedot ] };
+    key <AE08> { [            8,        asterisk,          dead_ogonek,        dead_horn ] };
+    key <AE09> { [            9,       parenleft,         dead_cedilla,        dead_hook ] };
+    key <AE10> { [            0,      parenright,          dead_macron,       dead_breve ] };
+    key <AE11> { [  bracketleft,       braceleft,          ordfeminine,       0x01000326 ] };
+    key <AE12> { [ bracketright,      braceright,            masculine,   dead_abovering ] };
+
+
+// Upper row
+    key <AD01> { [        slash,        question,               degree,     questiondown ] };
+    key <AD02> { [        comma,            less,           0x01000329,       0x01000313 ] };
+    key <AD03> { [       period,         greater,           0x01002022,   periodcentered ] };
+    key <AD04> { [            p,               P,                thorn,            THORN ] };
+    key <AD05> { [            y,               Y,                  yen,              yen ] };
+    key <AD06> { [            f,               F,  leftdoublequotemark,  leftsinglequotemark ] };
+    key <AD07> { [            g,               G, rightdoublequotemark, rightsinglequotemark ] };
+    key <AD08> { [            c,               C,              uparrow,        copyright ] };
+    key <AD09> { [            r,               R,           registered,       registered ] };
+    key <AD10> { [            l,               L,              lstroke,          Lstroke ] };
+    key <AD11> { [   dead_acute,      dead_grave,                acute,            grave ] };
+    key <AD12> { [        equal,            plus,              section,        plusminus ] };
+
+// Central row
+    key <AC01> { [            a,               A,                   ae,               AE ] };
+    key <AC02> { [            o,               O,                   oe,               OE ] };
+    key <AC03> { [            e,               E,             EuroSign,         EuroSign ] };
+    key <AC04> { [            u,               U,               oslash,           Oslash ] };
+    key <AC05> { [            i,               I,             idotless,        Iabovedot ] };
+    key <AC06> { [            d,               D,                  eth,              ETH ] };
+    key <AC07> { [            h,               H,            leftarrow,        paragraph ] };
+    key <AC08> { [            t,               T,           rightarrow,        trademark ] };
+    key <AC09> { [            n,               N,                  eng,              ENG ] };
+    key <AC10> { [            s,               S,               ssharp,            U1E9E ] };
+    key <AC11> { [   dead_tilde, dead_circumflex,           asciitilde,      asciicircum ] };
+    key <BKSL> { [        minus,      underscore,           0x01002015,       0x01000336 ] };
+
+// Lower row
+    key <LSGT> { [     ccedilla,        Ccedilla,            backslash,              bar ] };
+    key <AB01> { [    semicolon,           colon,           0x01000331,       0x0100032D ] };
+    key <AB02> { [            q,               Q,                schwa,            SCHWA ] };
+    key <AB03> { [            j,               J,                  ezh,              EZH ] };
+    key <AB04> { [            k,               K,        guillemotleft,       0x01002039 ] };
+    key <AB05> { [            x,               X,             multiply,         division ] };
+    key <AB06> { [            b,               B,       guillemotright,       0x0100203A ] };
+    key <AB07> { [            m,               M,            downarrow,               mu ] };
+    key <AB08> { [            w,               W,               ubreve,           Ubreve ] };
+    key <AB09> { [            v,               V,   doublelowquotemark, singlelowquotemark ] };
+    key <AB10> { [            z,               Z,           0x0100201F,       0x0100201B ] };
+    key <AB11> { [    backslash,             bar,             currency,        brokenbar ] };
+
+    key <SPCE> { [        space,           space,         nobreakspace,     nobreakspace ] };
+
+// Configures the "," for the numeric keypad
+    include "kpdl(comma)"
+
+// Configures the use of the AltGr key
+    include "level3(ralt_switch)"
+};

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# mado round 48 (C2; drafted by w1 seat ScrEntry, run by the L1) - read-only receipt generator for the XKB-compile knowledge entry.
+# Writes receipts/xkbcli-compile-keymap-r48.txt next to this script.
+# Every xkbcli call is a compile/test/kccgst verb: no compositor, no input device, no host
+# write. Probe fixtures live in a mktemp dir under the seat scratch root and are removed.
+set -u
+HERE=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
+OUT="$HERE/xkbcli-compile-keymap-r48.txt"
+K=/usr/bin/xkbcli
+S=${XKB_R48_SCRATCH:-/fast/scratch/hyaku/mado-r48/L1receipt}
+mkdir -p "$S"
+T=$(mktemp -d -p "$S" probe.XXXXXX) || exit 1
+trap 'rm -rf -- "$T"' EXIT INT TERM
+
+say() { printf '%s\n' "$*" >>"$OUT"; }
+cmd() { printf '$ %s\n' "$*" >>"$OUT"; }
+run() {  # run "<label>" <command...> : stdout+stderr+rc into the receipt
+    local label=$1; shift
+    say "--- $label"
+    cmd "$@"
+    local out rc
+    out=$("$@" 2>&1); rc=$?
+    printf '%s\n' "$out" | head -c 200000 >>"$OUT"
+    say "rc=$rc"
+    say ""
+}
+
+: >"$OUT"
+say "# mado round 48 - read-only xkbcli compile-keymap receipt (C2), generator .ufo/waves/round-48/receipts/make-xkb-receipt.sh"
+say "# taken: $(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ) (UTC)"
+say "# host: $(uname -srm)"
+say "# xkbcli: $K (no wrapper); compile/test/kccgst verbs only, stdout+stderr captured"
+say ""
+
+say "== versions, paths, defaults"
+run "xkbcli version" "$K" --version
+say '$ ls -d /var/lib/pacman/local/libxkbcommon-[0-9]* /var/lib/pacman/local/xkeyboard-config-[0-9]*   (package database read, no pacman run)'
+ls -d /var/lib/pacman/local/libxkbcommon-[0-9]* /var/lib/pacman/local/xkeyboard-config-[0-9]* >>"$OUT" 2>&1
+say "rc=$?"
+say ""
+run "canonical root" readlink -f /usr/share/X11/xkb
+say '$ ls -ld /usr/share/X11/xkb'
+ls -ld /usr/share/X11/xkb >>"$OUT" 2>&1
+say '$ ls /usr/share/xkeyboard-config-2/rules/'
+ls /usr/share/xkeyboard-config-2/rules/ >>"$OUT" 2>&1
+say ""
+
+say "== the host's names (read-only reads of the two input files the compositor loads)"
+say '$ grep -n "kb_" ~/.config/hypr/input.conf ~/.local/share/omarchy/default/hypr/input.conf'
+grep -n 'kb_' "$HOME/.config/hypr/input.conf" "$HOME/.local/share/omarchy/default/hypr/input.conf" >>"$OUT" 2>&1
+say '$ sha256sum the two files'
+sha256sum "$HOME/.config/hypr/input.conf" "$HOME/.local/share/omarchy/default/hypr/input.conf" >>"$OUT" 2>&1
+say ""
+
+say "== the names compile: full keymap digest (the keymap itself is ~73 KB and not kept)"
+say "\$ $K compile-keymap --layout br --options compose:caps > host.keymap"
+"$K" compile-keymap --layout br --options compose:caps >"$T/host.keymap" 2>"$T/host.err"
+say "rc=$?"
+say '$ sha256sum host.keymap'
+sha256sum "$T/host.keymap" | sed "s#$T/##" >>"$OUT"
+say '$ wc -lc host.keymap'
+wc -l <"$T/host.keymap" | awk '{print $1" lines"}' >>"$OUT"
+wc -c <"$T/host.keymap" | awk '{print $1" bytes"}' >>"$OUT"
+say '$ head -3 host.keymap'
+head -3 "$T/host.keymap" >>"$OUT"
+say '$ cat host.keymap.stderr (empty means no diagnostic at the default log level)'
+head -c 4000 "$T/host.err" >>"$OUT"
+say ""
+
+run "KcCGST for the host's names" "$K" compile-keymap --kccgst --layout br --options compose:caps
+run "host names, test mode (compiles, prints nothing)" "$K" compile-keymap --test --layout br --options compose:caps
+say "--- the include path list in order (verbose log, filtered to the path and KcCGST lines)"
+say '$ /usr/bin/xkbcli compile-keymap --test --verbose --layout br 2>&1 | grep -E "Include path|Include extensions|Compiling from"'
+"$K" compile-keymap --test --verbose --layout br 2>&1 \
+    | grep -E 'Include path|Include extensions|Compiling from' >>"$OUT"
+say "rc=${PIPESTATUS[0]}"
+say ""
+run "no names at all: the built-in defaults" "$K" compile-keymap --kccgst
+run "empty layout with a variant set" env XKB_LOG_LEVEL=warn "$K" compile-keymap --kccgst --layout '' --variant intl
+
+say "== unknown names: which hard-fail and which do not"
+run "unknown layout" "$K" compile-keymap --test --layout zz
+run "unknown variant on a known layout" "$K" compile-keymap --test --layout br --variant nope
+run "variant the br map does not define" "$K" compile-keymap --test --layout br --variant caps
+run "variant the br map defines" "$K" compile-keymap --test --layout br --variant nodeadkeys
+run "unknown option" "$K" compile-keymap --test --layout br --options nope:nope
+run "unknown model, KcCGST" "$K" compile-keymap --kccgst --layout br --model nope
+run "unknown model at XKB_LOG_LEVEL=warn (still silent: the model wildcard matched)" env XKB_LOG_LEVEL=warn "$K" compile-keymap --kccgst --layout br --model nope
+run "model pc105, KcCGST" "$K" compile-keymap --kccgst --layout br --model pc105
+run "unknown rules" "$K" compile-keymap --test --rules nope --layout br
+
+say "== rulesets that exist in the package"
+run "rules base" "$K" compile-keymap --kccgst --rules base --layout br
+run "rules xorg" "$K" compile-keymap --kccgst --rules xorg --layout br
+run "rules xfree98 (file exists, returns no components)" "$K" compile-keymap --test --rules xfree98 --layout br
+
+say "== multi-layout / variant alignment"
+run "two layouts, one variant (warning is below the default log level)" "$K" compile-keymap --kccgst --layout br,us --variant nodeadkeys
+run "the same run at XKB_LOG_LEVEL=warn" env XKB_LOG_LEVEL=warn "$K" compile-keymap --test --layout br,us --variant nodeadkeys
+run "one layout, two variants" "$K" compile-keymap --kccgst --layout br --variant nodeadkeys,intl
+run "two layouts, two variants (empty first)" "$K" compile-keymap --kccgst --layout br,us --variant ,dvorak
+
+say "== log level: the default prints ERROR-level lines only"
+run "unknown option at XKB_LOG_LEVEL=warn" env XKB_LOG_LEVEL=warn "$K" compile-keymap --test --layout br --options nope:nope
+say ""
+
+say "== include-path precedence (ephemeral probes; recreate with the recipe below)"
+say "# recipe: mkdir -p \$T/xdg/xkb/symbols and \$T/home/.xkb/symbols; write the 5-line symbols/mado48 below;"
+say "#   XDG_CONFIG_HOME=\$T/xdg xkbcli compile-keymap --layout mado48                 -> rc 0"
+say "#   XDG_CONFIG_HOME unset, HOME=\$T/home (file at \$HOME/.xkb/symbols/mado48)     -> rc 0"
+say "#   HOME=/nonexistent-home, XDG_CONFIG_HOME unset                            -> rc 1 (XKB-338)"
+say "# The adjudicating values are the exit status and the XKB-338 stderr block."
+cat >"$T/mado48.symbols" <<'EOF'
+default partial alphanumeric_keys
+xkb_symbols "basic" {
+    name[Group1]= "Precedence probe";
+    key <AE01> { [ 1 ] };
+};
+EOF
+say '$ cat symbols/mado48'
+cat "$T/mado48.symbols" >>"$OUT"
+mkdir -p "$T/xdg/xkb/symbols" "$T/home/.xkb/symbols"
+cp "$T/mado48.symbols" "$T/xdg/xkb/symbols/mado48"
+cp "$T/mado48.symbols" "$T/home/.xkb/symbols/mado48"
+XDG_CONFIG_HOME="$T/xdg" "$K" compile-keymap --layout mado48 >"$T/xdg-mado48.keymap" 2>"$T/xdg-mado48.err"
+say "XDG_CONFIG_HOME/xkb/symbols/mado48 present: rc=$?"
+say '$ sha256sum XDG-mado48.keymap'
+sha256sum "$T/xdg-mado48.keymap" | sed "s#$T/##" >>"$OUT"
+env -u XDG_CONFIG_HOME HOME="$T/home" "$K" compile-keymap --layout mado48 >"$T/home-mado48.keymap" 2>"$T/home-mado48.err"
+say "HOME/.xkb/symbols/mado48 present, XDG_CONFIG_HOME unset: rc=$?"
+say '$ cmp the two keymaps'
+if cmp -s "$T/xdg-mado48.keymap" "$T/home-mado48.keymap"; then say "byte-identical"; else say "differ"; fi
+env -u XDG_CONFIG_HOME HOME=/nonexistent-home "$K" compile-keymap --test --layout mado48 2>"$T/none-mado48.err"
+say "neither user path present: rc=$?"
+head -9 "$T/none-mado48.err" >>"$OUT"
+say ""
+run "root override falls back to the legacy root" env XKB_CONFIG_ROOT=/nonexistent-root XKB_LOG_LEVEL=warn "$K" compile-keymap --test --layout br
+run "extra-path override is only a failed include" env XKB_CONFIG_EXTRA_PATH=/nonexistent-extra "$K" compile-keymap --test --layout br
+say ""
+
+say "== xkbcli ignores the environment names unless the flag is given (compile-keymap.c:585-595)"
+run "XKB_DEFAULT_LAYOUT=zz, no flag" env XKB_DEFAULT_LAYOUT=zz "$K" compile-keymap --kccgst
+run "XKB_DEFAULT_LAYOUT=zz with --enable-environment-names" env XKB_DEFAULT_LAYOUT=zz "$K" compile-keymap --kccgst --enable-environment-names
+say "END OF RECEIPT"
+printf 'receipt written: %s\n' "$OUT"
+sha256sum "$OUT"
+printf 'bytes: %s\n' "$(wc -c <"$OUT")"

+# From `input:kb_*` names to a compiled keymap on this host: which XKB names libxkbcommon 1.13.2 accepts, which it drops, and which one fails the compile Hyprland 0.56.2 falls back on
+
+source: S340 - xkbcommon/libxkbcommon src/xkbcomp/rules.c @ xkbcommon-1.13.2 - the RMLVO matcher: comma lists, layout/variant pairing, `%l`/`%v` expansion, the "was ignored" report
+source: S341 - xkbcommon/libxkbcommon src/xkbcomp/xkbcomp.c @ xkbcommon-1.13.2 - the RMLVO -> KcCGST -> parse pipeline and its three failure messages
+source: S342 - xkbcommon/libxkbcommon src/xkbcomp/include.c @ xkbcommon-1.13.2 - `symbols/<name>` lookup over the include paths, XKB-338 and XKB-661
+source: S343 - xkbcommon/libxkbcommon src/context.c @ xkbcommon-1.13.2 - the default include-path order, the legacy-root fallback, the default log level
+source: S344 - xkbcommon/libxkbcommon src/context-priv.c @ xkbcommon-1.13.2 - empty names replaced by defaults, layout-and-variant tie
+source: S345 - xkbcommon/libxkbcommon meson_options.txt @ xkbcommon-1.13.2 - the compiled-in default rules/model/layout/variant/options
+source: S346 - xkeyboard-config 2.48 rules/evdev (installed at /usr/share/xkeyboard-config-2/rules/evdev; generated from rules/rules.in by xkeyboard-config-rules-meson.build:18-39) - the wildcard sections that turn names into KcCGST
+source: S347 - xkeyboard-config 2.48 symbols/br (tag xkeyboard-config-2.48, byte-equal to the host file) - the eleven maps `br` offers and the map names a variant must match
+source: S348 - xkbcommon/libxkbcommon tools/compile-keymap.c @ xkbcommon-1.13.2 - what `xkbcli compile-keymap` passes, what `--test` skips, the exit status
+source: S349 - xkbcommon/libxkbcommon src/messages-codes.h @ xkbcommon-1.13.2 - the numeric ids XKB-338/595/661/769/822
+source: S350 - mado host, read-only `xkbcli compile-keymap` receipt (own-work) - this host's names, the five unknown-name classes, the include-path precedence probes
+verified-at: 2026-10-26
+recheck: on-release - when libxkbcommon leaves 1.13.2 or xkeyboard-config leaves 2.48, re-pull the nine upstream code pins and re-run `.ufo/waves/round-48/tools/make-xkb-receipt.sh`; the ruleset the host reads is generated (`xkeyboard-config-rules-meson.build:18-39`), so a package rebuild can change the wildcards without any change in the tag's `rules/rules.in`
+ground: the XKB compile under the input plane, held open at base - `corpus/knowledge/hyprland-input-devices-r46.md:69` ("The XKB compile itself is not reproduced") and `corpus/README.md:112` ("Still open: the XKB compile itself"); held neighbours: rows S318/S319 give the compositor's `us` default and its fallback after a keymap that does not compile, cited here and never re-claimed
+Lane: mado-shokunin. Round: 48.
+
+## What the evidence licenses
+
+| question | where the answer lives | state |
+|---|---|---|
+| which `kb_rules` names exist on this host, and what a name that does not exist does | step 3, host table | observed 2026-10-26 |
+| which `kb_layout` / `kb_variant` pairs compile, and which fail | steps 4-6, host table | observed |
+| which unknown names are dropped with a message and which fail the compile | step 7, host table | observed |
+| how a comma list in `kb_layout` pairs with a comma list in `kb_variant` | step 2, step 5 | sourced and observed |
+| where a name is searched for, and which path wins | step 6 | sourced, precedence observed |
+| what "unknown model" does when it prints nothing | step 7 | observed (the KcCGST geometry changes) |
+| why a keyboard ends up on `us` after a bad name | step 8 | held: rows S318/S319, not re-run here |
+| which diagnostics a failed or degraded compile prints by itself | step 9 | observed (log-level dependent) |
+| whether the compositor's own context honours `XKB_DEFAULT_*` | step 1 | sourced (held row S319, its context line); not run against the live compositor |
+
+## The compile, step by step
+
+1. **Names and defaults.** Before any file is read, an empty rules, model or layout string is replaced (xkbcommon-context-priv.c:192-217): by `XKB_DEFAULT_RULES`, `XKB_DEFAULT_MODEL` or `XKB_DEFAULT_LAYOUT` when the context reads the environment (xkbcommon-context-priv.c:129-160), else by the compiled-in rules `evdev`, model `pc105` and layout `us` (xkbcommon-meson_options.txt:32-59, confirmed live by a run with no names). Layout and variant are tied: with an empty layout the variant is discarded and both come from the defaults, with a warning that the default level hides (xkbcommon-context-priv.c:202-217). Options are replaced only when the caller passes none at all: `Options can be empty, so respect that if passed in` at xkbcommon-context-priv.c:218. Hyprland builds every keyboard keymap in a context made with `XKB_CONTEXT_NO_FLAGS` (Hyprland-IKeyboard.cpp:75, held row S319), so the compositor's context does read the environment, and it passes the five names as C strings, never as NULL (Hyprland-IKeyboard.cpp:67-73): an empty `kb_layout` would take `XKB_DEFAULT_LAYOUT` from the compositor's environment, while an empty `kb_options` stays empty. `xkbcli` disables the environment route unless `--enable-environment-names` is given (xkbcommon-tools-compile-keymap.c:585-587), so a receipt taken from `xkbcli` proves the names on its command line and nothing else; with the flag, `XKB_DEFAULT_LAYOUT=zz` reaches the rules (receipt).
+2. **Comma lists.** The layout string is split on commas into one entry per group and the variant string likewise; a shorter variant list is zero-padded (xkbcommon-rules.c:520-527) and a longer one is truncated with an ERROR-level line (xkbcommon-rules.c:528-535). Both were run: `br,us` with `nodeadkeys` yields `pc+br(nodeadkeys)+us:2`, `br` with `nodeadkeys,intl` yields `pc+br(nodeadkeys)` plus `Less layouts than variants`.
+3. **The rules file.** The ruleset name is resolved as `rules/<name>` over the include paths; a missing file stops the resolve at once (xkbcommon-rules.c:1986-1992). Optional `<name>.pre` and `<name>.post` files are read around it, one per include path, in path order (xkbcommon-rules.c:1995-2025). The package installs four rulesets - `base`, `evdev`, `xorg`, `xfree98` (receipt `ls`) - and `evdev` is the default. A file that exists can still resolve nothing: `xfree98` returns no components for this layout and the compile fails (xkbcommon-rules.c:2028-2036, receipt rc 1).
+4. **Names to components.** `rules/evdev` maps the names with wildcard sections: any model to keycodes `evdev` (`xkeyboard-config-rules-evdev:55-59`), any layout to `+aliases(qwerty)` (:61-64), any model+layout+variant to symbols `pc+%l%(v)` (:109-175), and each option name explicitly to its symbols include (:529-586). So the host's `br` + `compose:caps` becomes keycodes `evdev+aliases(qwerty)`, types and compat `complete`, symbols `pc+br+inet(evdev)+compose(caps)`, geometry `pc(pc105)` (receipt).
+5. **`%l`/`%v` expansion and pairing.** A `%l` or `%v` with no index expands only when a single layout (variant) was given; with several, the index must be written or the `%i` group index used (xkbcommon-rules.c:1257-1279). Expansion marks the name matched (xkbcommon-rules.c:1297) - which is why an unknown layout produces no "ignored" line, but a symbols file name that does not exist. A variant is always expanded next to its layout, and the symbols value carries the group index for every layout after the first (`us:2`, receipt).
+6. **File and map lookup.** The generated component names are resolved as `symbols/<file>` over the include paths, first hit wins over the ordered list (xkbcommon-include.c:333); the order is `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/xkb` (else `$HOME/.config/xkb`), `$HOME/.xkb`, `$XKB_CONFIG_EXTRA_PATH` (`/etc/xkb`), the versioned then unversioned extension directories, and `$XKB_CONFIG_ROOT` (`/usr/share/xkeyboard-config-2`) last (xkbcommon-context.c:243-338). A required file that no path has logs XKB-338 with a census of the paths searched and the paths that could not be added (xkbcommon-include.c:353-358); a file that exists but carries no map of that name logs XKB-661 with the include statement (xkbcommon-include.c:498-506). If the configured root cannot be appended, the legacy root `/usr/share/X11/xkb` is tried instead, silently at the default log level (xkbcommon-context.c:327-337). Precedence is live: a five-line `symbols/zz` placed at `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/xkb` or `$HOME/.xkb` makes the otherwise fatal `zz` compile (rc 0, byte-identical keymaps), and without either path the same run fails XKB-338 (receipt).
+7. **What each name class does when it is wrong.** Rules: a missing name fails; an existing name with no components fails. Model: every string matches the model wildcards, so an unknown model compiles - `--model nope` returns keycodes `evdev` and geometry `pc(pc104)` instead of `pc(pc105)`, and prints nothing even at `XKB_LOG_LEVEL=warn`, because a wildcard matched and there is nothing to report (the only trace is the geometry). Layout: the wildcard matches, `%l` substitutes the name into the symbols file, and a name with no `symbols/<name>` file fails the compile: `--layout zz` gives XKB-338 `symbols/zz`, XKB-661 `zz`, XKB-769, XKB-822, rc 1. Variant: the name is expanded into `symbols/<layout>(<variant>)`, so a variant the layout file does not define fails the same way: `br(nope)` and `br(caps)` each give XKB-661 and rc 1, while `br(nodeadkeys)` - a map the file has - compiles. Options: the option sections carry no wildcard (`xkeyboard-config-rules-evdev:529-586`), so an unmatched option is reported and dropped: `nope:nope` gives XKB-595 `Unrecognized RMLVO option "nope:nope" was ignored` and rc 0. The report itself is emitted after a successful resolve for any unmatched model, layout, variant or option (xkbcommon-rules.c:2045-2064).
+8. **What the compositor does with a failed compile.** A resolve or parse failure makes the RMLVO call return NULL (xkbcommon-xkbcomp.c:157-166 for the resolve, xkbcommon-xkbcomp.c:60-64 for the parse), which is the only thing Hyprland sees. Hyprland starts from `us` when no layout is configured (S318, `input:kb_layout` default) and resets a keyboard whose names did not compile to layout `us` with every other name empty (S319, the `m_currentRules` reset after a failed keymap). Both are held from round 46 (`hyprland-input-devices-r46.md:33`, its step 4) and are not re-measured here; this entry supplies only the libxkbcommon half of that chain.
+9. **Which diagnostics are visible.** The default log level is ERROR (xkbcommon-context.c:503), so WARNING lines are dropped unless `XKB_LOG_LEVEL=warn` (or `--verbose`). A layout/variant count mismatch is a warning and therefore invisible by default; the "was ignored" line for an unmatched option is ERROR-level and therefore visible even though the compile succeeds. The receipt carries the same run at both levels.
+
+## Observations
+
+**OBS** S340 (pin opened 2026-10-26): `No index provided: match only if single layout` at xkbcommon-rules.c:1259 - `%l` expands only for a one-element layout list.
+**OBS** S340: `expanded_value->matched = true;` at xkbcommon-rules.c:1297 - a name consumed by an expansion counts as matched, so step 7's layout case prints no "ignored" line.
+**OBS** S340: `Unrecognized RMLVO option \"%.*s\" was ignored` at xkbcommon-rules.c:2063 - reported with code XKB-595, after a resolve that succeeded.
+**OBS** S340: `Less layouts than variants: \"%s\" vs. \"%s\".` at xkbcommon-rules.c:530 - the truncation branch's message.
+**OBS** S341: `ok = xkb_components_from_rules_names(keymap->ctx, rmlvo, &kccgst,` at xkbcommon-xkbcomp.c:157 - the resolve that decides between a compile and an error.
+**OBS** S341: `Couldn't look up rules '%s', model '%s', layout '%s', ` at xkbcommon-xkbcomp.c:161 - the message that carries the rejected names.
+**OBS** S342: `Couldn't find file \"%s/%.*s\" in include paths` at xkbcommon-include.c:355 - the XKB-338 line shape, `symbols/zz` for an unknown layout.
+**OBS** S342: `Couldn't process include statement for '%s(%s)'` at xkbcommon-include.c:501 - the XKB-661 line shape, `br(nope)` for an unknown variant.
+**OBS** S343: `const char * const root = xkb_context_include_path_get_system_path(ctx);` at xkbcommon-context.c:317 - the root is appended last.
+**OBS** S343: `ret |= context_include_path_append(ctx, DFLT_XKB_LEGACY_ROOT);` at xkbcommon-context.c:336 - the legacy-root fallback behind a failed root.
+**OBS** S343: `ctx->log_level = XKB_LOG_LEVEL_ERROR;` at xkbcommon-context.c:503 - the default level that hides every WARNING line.
+**OBS** S344: `Layout not provided, but variant set to \"%s\": ` at xkbcommon-context-priv.c:208 - an empty layout discards the variant.
+**OBS** S345: `value: 'us',` at xkbcommon-meson_options.txt:46 - the compiled-in default layout (`default-layout` at :44).
+**OBS** S346: `pc+%l%(v)` at xkeyboard-config-rules-evdev:175 - the catch-all symbols value; the layout and variant names are the file and map names.
+**OBS** S346: `compose:caps` at xkeyboard-config-rules-evdev:570 - the option that the host's config uses, mapped to `+compose(caps)`.
+**OBS** S347: `xkb_symbols "nodeadkeys" {` at xkeyboard-config-symbols-br:47 - one of the eleven maps `br` offers; `nope` and `caps` are not among them.
+**OBS** S348: `ctx_flags |= XKB_CONTEXT_NO_ENVIRONMENT_NAMES;` at xkbcommon-tools-compile-keymap.c:587 - `xkbcli` ignores `XKB_DEFAULT_*` unless the flag is given.
+**OBS** S348: `fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: Couldn't create xkb keymap\n");` at xkbcommon-tools-compile-keymap.c:521 - the tool's own line, after libxkbcommon's.
+**OBS** S349: `XKB_ERROR_INCLUDED_FILE_NOT_FOUND = 338,` at xkbcommon-messages-codes.h:116 and `XKB_ERROR_CANNOT_RESOLVE_RMLVO = 595,` at :150 - the two ids that name the drop classes.
+
+Census of the pinned bytes:
+
+compile_defaults rules=evdev model=pc105 layout=us variant=empty options=empty include_paths=4 base(+2 extension dirs) rules_files=4 br_maps=11 msg_ids=338,595,661,769,822 max_rules_include_depth=5

+## Live host run (read-only)
+
+The host's names are `kb_layout = br` and `kb_options = compose:caps` in `~/.config/hypr/input.conf` (sha256 `3769c92ab7660f1795cb6d0af1bcc5a5614a299b29ae8358d1b85936bdd47bf4`, lines 10-11), after the omarchy default input file (`bee5aa68cbec0c428fae7a5faf7da49a30e6f78ed96e78a063f08b66942448b8`, `kb_layout = us` at line 3) that `hyprland.conf` sources first - the file order recorded in the round-46 erratum. `xkbcli compile-keymap --layout br --options compose:caps` returns rc 0, prints nothing on stderr, and produces a keymap of 73079 bytes / 2090 lines with sha256 `efd487a399201523540f4e5bbf81818be3183f6d9957c2f2b12469a2cf6fce81` (receipt `.ufo/waves/round-48/receipts/xkbcli-compile-keymap-r48.txt`). Its KcCGST line is `pc+br+inet(evdev)+compose(caps)`.
+
+The verbose log shows the search order step 6 predicts: `~/.config/xkb`, `~/.xkb` and `/etc/xkb` could not be added, the two extension directories `/usr/share/xkeyboard-config-2.d` and `/usr/share/xkeyboard-config.d` do not exist, and `/usr/share/xkeyboard-config-2` is the one include path added (receipt).
+
+The five name classes, each run on this host against libxkbcommon 1.13.2-1 and xkeyboard-config 2.48-1 (receipt):
+
+| probe | stdout / stderr | exit |
+|---|---|---|
+| `--rules base`, `--rules xorg` | compiles, symbols `pc+br+inet(pc105)` | rc 0 |
+| `--rules xfree98` | XKB-595 `No components returned from XKB rules "xfree98"` | rc 1 |
+| `--rules nope` | XKB-338 `rules/nope`, XKB-595 `Cannot load XKB rules "nope"`, XKB-822 | rc 1 |
+| `--model nope` | silent at every level; geometry `pc(pc104)` instead of `pc(pc105)` | rc 0 |
+| `--layout br` | compiles | rc 0 |
+| `--layout zz` | XKB-338 `symbols/zz`, XKB-661 `zz`, XKB-769, XKB-822 | rc 1 |
+| `--variant nodeadkeys` (on `br`) | compiles | rc 0 |
+| `--variant nope`, `--variant caps` (on `br`) | XKB-661 `br(nope)` / `br(caps)`, XKB-769, XKB-822 | rc 1 |
+| `--options compose:caps` | compiles, `+compose(caps)` | rc 0 |
+| `--options nope:nope` | XKB-595 `Unrecognized RMLVO option "nope:nope" was ignored` | rc 0 |
+
+Multi-layout: `--layout br,us --variant nodeadkeys` gives symbols `pc+br(nodeadkeys)+us:2+inet(evdev)` and only a WARNING about the missing second variant (visible at `XKB_LOG_LEVEL=warn`); `--layout br --variant nodeadkeys,intl` drops the second variant with an ERROR-level line and still exits 0; `--layout br,us --variant ,dvorak` pairs `dvorak` with `us`. `--layout '' --variant intl` discards the variant and compiles `us`.
+
+## Reading a failure
+
+| symptom | most likely cause | check |
+|---|---|---|
+| keyboard prints `us`, config says another layout | compile failed and the compositor reset it (step 8) | `xkbcli compile-keymap --test --layout <name> --variant <v> --options <o>`; rc 1 names the class |
+| `Couldn't find file "symbols/<name>"` | layout name has no symbols file on any include path | `ls /usr/share/xkeyboard-config-2/symbols/<name>`; or the file is in a user path that is not on the list (step 6) |
+| `Couldn't process include statement for '<layout>(<variant>)'` | variant is not a map in that layout's symbols file | `grep -n '^xkb_symbols' /usr/share/xkeyboard-config-2/symbols/<layout>` |
+| `Cannot load XKB rules "<name>"` | no `rules/<name>` in any include path | `ls /usr/share/xkeyboard-config-2/rules/` |
+| `No components returned from XKB rules "<name>"` | the ruleset exists but maps nothing for these names | `xkbcli compile-keymap --kccgst --rules <name> --layout <l>` |
+| `Unrecognized RMLVO option ... was ignored`, rc 0 | option typo; the compile succeeds without it | compare against `[[:space:]]<option>[[:space:]]*=.*symbols` in `rules/evdev` |
+| no diagnostic at all, but the keymap looks wrong | an empty or defaulted name (step 1), or a WARNING hidden by the default log level (step 9) | re-run with `XKB_LOG_LEVEL=warn` |
+| user file in `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/xkb` or `$HOME/.xkb` has no effect | an earlier path supplied the file, or the name is not the file the rules generate | the XKB-338 census lists the paths searched, in order |
+
+## Limits
+
+- The compositor half is held, not measured: no Hyprland restart, reload or keymap dump was taken this round; steps 8 cite S318/S319. Hyprland's context reads the environment (step 1), but the compositor's own environment was not read this round, so whether any `XKB_DEFAULT_*` is set for it is [UNK]; whether its libxkbcommon build enables extension directories is [UNK].
+- `kb_file` bypasses this whole chain - a keymap file is parsed directly, so a readable `kb_file` makes every table here about names moot (round-46 step 4, held).
+- Options that land in compat or types rather than symbols (`grp:*`, `caps:escape*`, `lv3:*` and the keypad options) were not enumerated; only `compose:caps` and one unknown option were run. The option sections of `rules/evdev` are the place to extend this.
+- Per-layout variant lists are not enumerated: `br` has eleven maps, and the same check on another layout needs its own `symbols/<layout>` read. No layout other than `br`, `us` and the synthetic `zz` was compiled.
+- The keymap body itself is not archived (about 73 KB); only its digest, byte count and line count are held, so a byte-level comparison of two builds is possible but a semantic diff was not run.
+- The ruleset the host reads is generated at package build time; the tag's `rules/rules.in` and the generator inputs were not re-run here, so the mapping from `rules.in` to the installed `rules/evdev` is [UNK] beyond the meson target's name.
+- Only `xkbcli` was executed. The compositor links its own libxkbcommon build; the package is the same 1.13.2-1, but build options (extra include paths, extension directories) are [UNK] for that binary.
+- The two user include paths were probed in a scratch `XDG_CONFIG_HOME`/`HOME`, not in the real ones; the host's own `~/.config/xkb` and `~/.xkb` do not exist (receipt, XKB-338 census), so their absence is a host fact but their behaviour is inferred from the scratch probe.
